            border-radius: 25px;
            font-weight: bold;
            font-size: 16px;
        }

        .footer #floater>div,
        .footer #feedback,
        .footer .open-egain-chat-button,
        .footer .open-egain-chat-button:before{
            color: #1a1552;
        }
        
        .footer #floater>div:hover,
        .footer #feedback:hover,
        .footer .open-egain-chat-button:hover,
        .footer .open-egain-chat-button:hover:before{
            background-color: #f5f5f5;
            color: #1a1552;
        }


        .footer .qq-chat .qq-bg,
        .footer #feedback:before,
        .footer .open-egain-chat-button:before {
            height: 25px;
            display: inline-block;
            padding: 0px;
            width: 25px;
            vertical-align: middle
        }

        .footer .qq-chat .qq-text {
            display: inline-block;
            padding: 0 10px;
        }
    }
